event = { #Oil shortages
	id = 30001
	random = no
	persistent = yes
	#invisible
	#TAG = { AFG ALB ALG ANG ARA ARG ARM AST AUS AZB BEL BEN BHU BLR BOL BOS BRA BRU BUL BUR CAL CAM CAN CGX CHC CHI CHL CMB COL CON COS CRO CSA CSX CUB CXB CYN CYP CZE DDR DEN DFR DOM EAF ECU EGY ENG EQA EST ETH EUS FIN FLA VIC GAB GEO GER GLD GRE GUA GUI GUY HAI HOL HON HUN ICL IDC IND INO IRE IRQ ISR ITA JAP JOR KAZ KOR KUR KYG LAO LAT LBY LEB LIB LIT LUX MAD MAL MAN MEN MEX MLY MON MOR MOZ MTN NAM NEP NIC NIG NOR NZL OMN OTT PAK PAL PAN PAR PER PHI POL POR PRI PRK PRU QUE RHO ROM RSI RUS SAF SAL SAR SAU SCA SCH SCO SER SIA SIB SIE SIK SLO SLV SOM SOV SPA SPR SUD SWE SYR TAJ TAN TEX TIB TRA TRK TUN TUR UKR URU USA UZB VEN VIE WLL YEM YUG U01 U02 U03 U04 U05 U06 U08 U09 U10 U11 U12 U13 U14 U15 U16 U17 U18 U19 U20 U21 U22 U23 U24 U25 U26 U27 U28 U29 U30 U31 U32 U33 U34 U35 U36 U39 U40 U41 U42 U43 U44 U45 U46 U47 U48 U49 U50 U51 U52 U53 U54 U55 U56 U57 U58 U59 U60 U61 U62 U63 U64 U65 U66 U67 U68 U69 U70 U71 U72 U73 U74 U75 U76 U77 U78 U79 U80 U81 U82 U83 U84 U85 U86 U87 U88 U89 U90 U91 U92 U93 U94 U95 U96 U97 U98 U99 USN UIC UTL UAU UPS UCH UBO UCS URO UER UGS UPE UTC }
	
	trigger = {	
		local_flag = AllInit
		NOT = { local_flag = shortagesOil }
		policy = { type = free_market value = 2 }
		ic = 5
		NOT = { oil = 100 }
	}
   
	name = "Oil shortages"
	desc = "Our fuel stockpile is getting critically low. Our economy needs oil to function properly - factories, transport, and trade will start to stall if the situation is not urgently addressed. We have 15 days to secure at least 100 oil in our stockpile or face blackouts, idle factories, and economic decline. Trade, conquer, or negotiate - before the lights go out."
	picture = "mds_supplies1"
	style = 2
	
	date = { day = 0 month = february year = 1936 }
	offset = 3
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = "Where's the fuel!?!"
		command = { type = local_setflag which = shortagesOil }
		command = { type = event which = 30002 where = -1 when = 15 }
		command = { type = event which = 30003 where = -1 when = 15 }
	}
}

	id = 30002
	random = no
	persistent = yes
	#visible
	TAG = { AFG ALB ALG ANG ARA ARG ARM AST AUS AZB BEL BEN BHU BLR BOL BOS BRA BRU BUL BUR CAL CAM CAN CGX CHC CHI CHL CMB COL CON COS CRO CSA CSX CUB CXB CYN CYP CZE DDR DEN DFR DOM EAF ECU EGY ENG EQA EST ETH EUS FIN FLA VIC GAB GEO GER GLD GRE GUA GUI GUY HAI HOL HON HUN ICL IDC IND INO IRE IRQ ISR ITA JAP JOR KAZ KOR KUR KYG LAO LAT LBY LEB LIB LIT LUX MAD MAL MAN MEN MEX MLY MON MOR MOZ MTN NAM NEP NIC NIG NOR NZL OMN OTT PAK PAL PAN PAR PER PHI POL POR PRI PRK PRU QUE RHO ROM RSI RUS SAF SAL SAR SAU SCA SCH SCO SER SIA SIB SIE SIK SLO SLV SOM SOV SPA SPR SUD SWE SYR TAJ TAN TEX TIB TRA TRK TUN TUR UKR URU USA UZB VEN VIE WLL YEM YUG U01 U02 U03 U04 U05 U06 U08 U09 U10 U11 U12 U13 U14 U15 U16 U17 U18 U19 U20 U21 U22 U23 U24 U25 U26 U27 U28 U29 U30 U31 U32 U33 U34 U35 U36 U39 U40 U41 U42 U43 U44 U45 U46 U47 U48 U49 U50 U51 U52 U53 U54 U55 U56 U57 U58 U59 U60 U61 U62 U63 U64 U65 U66 U67 U68 U69 U70 U71 U72 U73 U74 U75 U76 U77 U78 U79 U80 U81 U82 U83 U84 U85 U86 U87 U88 U89 U90 U91 U92 U93 U94 U95 U96 U97 U98 U99 USN UIC UTL UAU UPS UCH UBO UCS URO UER UGS UPE UTC }
	
	name = "Oil situation worsens"
	desc = "Our failure to secure additional oil has led to fuel rationing and reduced productivity. Factories operate at reduced capacity, shipping delays are mounting, and consumer prices are rising - but our economy persists, sluggish yet stable. We must adapt quickly or face prolonged stagnation."
	picture = "mds_supplies2"
	style = 2
	
	action = {
		name = "Where's the fuel!?!"
		trigger = {
			ic = 5
			NOT = { oil = 100 }
		}

		command = { trigger = { policy = { type = free_market value = 2 } NOT = { policy = { type = free_market value = 3 } } }		type = dissent 								value = 0.5	}
		command = { trigger = { policy = { type = free_market value = 3 } NOT = { policy = { type = free_market value = 4 } } }		type = dissent 								value = 1.0	}
		command = { trigger = { policy = { type = free_market value = 4 } NOT = { policy = { type = free_market value = 5 } } }		type = dissent 								value = 1.5	}
		command = { trigger = { policy = { type = free_market value = 5 } NOT = { policy = { type = free_market value = 6 } } }		type = dissent 								value = 2.0	}
		command = { trigger = { policy = { type = free_market value = 6 } NOT = { policy = { type = free_market value = 7 } } }		type = dissent 								value = 2.5	}
		command = { trigger = { policy = { type = free_market value = 7 } NOT = { policy = { type = free_market value = 8 } } }		type = dissent 								value = 3.0	}
		command = { trigger = { policy = { type = free_market value = 8 } NOT = { policy = { type = free_market value = 9 } } }		type = dissent 								value = 3.5	}
		command = { trigger = { policy = { type = free_market value = 9 } NOT = { policy = { type = free_market value = 10 } } }	type = dissent 								value = 4.0	}
		command = { trigger = { policy = { type = free_market value = 10 } 													  }		type = dissent 								value = 4.5	}
		
		command = { trigger = { policy = { type = free_market value = 2 } NOT = { policy = { type = free_market value = 3 } } }		type = industrial_modifier which = total	value = -5	}
		command = { trigger = { policy = { type = free_market value = 3 } NOT = { policy = { type = free_market value = 4 } } }		type = industrial_modifier which = total	value = -10	}
		command = { trigger = { policy = { type = free_market value = 4 } NOT = { policy = { type = free_market value = 5 } } }		type = industrial_modifier which = total	value = -15	}
		command = { trigger = { policy = { type = free_market value = 5 } NOT = { policy = { type = free_market value = 6 } } }		type = industrial_modifier which = total	value = -20	}
		command = { trigger = { policy = { type = free_market value = 6 } NOT = { policy = { type = free_market value = 7 } } }		type = industrial_modifier which = total	value = -25	}
		command = { trigger = { policy = { type = free_market value = 7 } NOT = { policy = { type = free_market value = 8 } } }		type = industrial_modifier which = total	value = -30	}
		command = { trigger = { policy = { type = free_market value = 8 } NOT = { policy = { type = free_market value = 9 } } }		type = industrial_modifier which = total	value = -35	}
		command = { trigger = { policy = { type = free_market value = 9 } NOT = { policy = { type = free_market value = 10 } } }	type = industrial_modifier which = total	value = -40	}
		command = { trigger = { policy = { type = free_market value = 10 } 													  }		type = industrial_modifier which = total	value = -45	}
		
		command = { type = local_setflag which = shortagesOilCounter when = 1 value = 1 }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = shortagesOil }
	}
}
event = { #Oil shortages
	id = 30003
	random = no
	persistent = yes
	#invisible
	#TAG = { AFG ALB ALG ANG ARA ARG ARM AST AUS AZB BEL BEN BHU BLR BOL BOS BRA BRU BUL BUR CAL CAM CAN CGX CHC CHI CHL CMB COL CON COS CRO CSA CSX CUB CXB CYN CYP CZE DDR DEN DFR DOM EAF ECU EGY ENG EQA EST ETH EUS FIN FLA VIC GAB GEO GER GLD GRE GUA GUI GUY HAI HOL HON HUN ICL IDC IND INO IRE IRQ ISR ITA JAP JOR KAZ KOR KUR KYG LAO LAT LBY LEB LIB LIT LUX MAD MAL MAN MEN MEX MLY MON MOR MOZ MTN NAM NEP NIC NIG NOR NZL OMN OTT PAK PAL PAN PAR PER PHI POL POR PRI PRK PRU QUE RHO ROM RSI RUS SAF SAL SAR SAU SCA SCH SCO SER SIA SIB SIE SIK SLO SLV SOM SOV SPA SPR SUD SWE SYR TAJ TAN TEX TIB TRA TRK TUN TUR UKR URU USA UZB VEN VIE WLL YEM YUG U01 U02 U03 U04 U05 U06 U08 U09 U10 U11 U12 U13 U14 U15 U16 U17 U18 U19 U20 U21 U22 U23 U24 U25 U26 U27 U28 U29 U30 U31 U32 U33 U34 U35 U36 U39 U40 U41 U42 U43 U44 U45 U46 U47 U48 U49 U50 U51 U52 U53 U54 U55 U56 U57 U58 U59 U60 U61 U62 U63 U64 U65 U66 U67 U68 U69 U70 U71 U72 U73 U74 U75 U76 U77 U78 U79 U80 U81 U82 U83 U84 U85 U86 U87 U88 U89 U90 U91 U92 U93 U94 U95 U96 U97 U98 U99 USN UIC UTL UAU UPS UCH UBO UCS URO UER UGS UPE UTC }
	
	name = "Oil situation improves"
	desc = "Oir swift actions have replenished critical fuel reserves, keeping factories humming and supply chains moving. The economy regains momentum, with productivity stabilizing and fuel prices easing. The threat of slowdown fades as trucks return to highways and power plants roar back to full capacity."
	picture = "mds_supplies4"
	style = 2
	
	action = {
		name = "The situation stabilizes, for now"
		trigger = {
			local_flag = shortagesOil
			OR = {
				NOT = { ic = 5 }
				oil = 100
			}
				
		}
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = shortagesOil }
	}
}
